B001 makes each writing an achieved assembly rather than an inert object. **Abductive move:** I infer that the material nesting models coherent plurality and that the following human scattering is a contrast to it. **Reading change:** `كُتُب` reads as organized joinings whose form silently tests whether a receiving community can remain joined.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001283/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6818193794665696e0af","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:3:2\",\"root_001283\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu yazılar, sonraki emir ve karşılıkla birlikte, yalnızca değerli metinler değil bağlayıcı ve uygulanabilir hükümler olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000244/B001","root_001273/B004","root_001273/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_842ff37f421529c07e54","text":"98:5 was the lexical surprise: `يُقِيمُوا` and `دِينُ ٱلْقَيِّمَة` turned fixed `ق و م` B004 and B009 from qualities of writings into practices of maintenance. 98:6–98:7 then made B010’s valuation reader-facing through the paired `شَرّ/خَيْر ٱلْبَرِيَّة`, and 98:8’s matching `ج ز ي` B001 supplied the retrospective consequence of B003’s binding-decree reading for fixed `ك ت ب`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_86addb157144eafb68f7","text":"98:5’s “ذلك دين القيّمة” was the main surprise: قيمة in 98:3 is not only a property of texts but becomes the name of an enacted religion. 98:4 then made the ordered plurality of كتب contrast with social تفرّق.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000244/B001","root_001273/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8cba49b7646fcb57c16c","text":"4. **A standard that appraises its readers.** **Lexical evidence:** `قَيِّمَة` activates `ق و م` B010, “value, valuation, and appraisal,” with B015, “equal weight and even measure,” as a secondary image. **Structural cue and role:** 98:6–98:7 rank two responses as `شَرّ` and `خَيْر` of the same `بَرِيَّة`, and 98:8 gives a recompense matching action (`ج ز ي` B001). The writings therefore function as an evaluative measure, not only as objects possessing value. **Abductive move:** I infer that the disclosed order supplies the criterion behind the later appraisal. **Reading change:** `قَيِّمَة` becomes reader-facing: these writings establish value by weighing what recipients do after they receive clarity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B004","root_001273/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9711417569f9f1829ac0","text":"2. **Writings that maintain an order.** **Lexical evidence:** `قَيِّمَة` anchors `ق و م` B004, “care, guardianship, and preservation,” and B009, “support, mainstay, and subsistence,” alongside B008, uprightness. **Structural cue and role:** 98:5 repeats the root in `يُقِيمُوا` and `دِينُ ٱلْقَيِّمَة`; prayer is maintained between those repetitions, and giving is coupled to it. The writings’ quality thus migrates into sustained practices that hold an order up. **Abductive move:** I infer that root repetition maps textual maintenance onto human maintenance; the packet supplies the recurrence but not the transfer rule. **Reading change:** `قَيِّمَة` means more than correct or straight: the writings are custodial supports meant to keep a form of life standing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B007","root_001273/B008","root_001283/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9763b5b727ffd3377a7d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:3:2\",\"root_001283\",[\"B001\"]],[\"98:3:3\",\"root_001273\",[\"B007\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İçerideki çoğul yazılar, birbirine eklenmiş ve birbirini ayakta tutan düzenli bir bütün oluşturur; bu bütünlük alıcıların sonraki dağılmasını ölçer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B008","root_001283/B002","root_001283/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bd27fbff41434f6265f7","text":"1. **Inside the sheets: written decrees that stand upright.** Evidence: ك ت ب B002 writing/book and B003 binding decree; ق و م B008 straightness/uprightness, B004 care/maintenance, B009 mainstay/support. Structural cue: 98:2 has صحفا مطهرة; 98:5 repeats دين القيمة and يقيموا الصلاة. Assigned role: كتب are the contents that bind; قيمة names their rectifying, sustaining posture. Abductive move: I infer that the “books” are not merely textual units but prescriptions that make a way stand. Reading change: the ayah reads as “inside them are stabilizing ordinances,” not only “valuable writings.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001283/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bdbea20dc37d36fa838f","text":"3. **Contents with directive force.** **Lexical evidence:** `كُتُب` also activates `ك ت ب` B003, “inscribing as binding decree or judgment.” **Structural cue and role:** the terse nominal description is followed by `أُمِرُوا` in 98:5 and by matched recompense in 98:8. The decree branch assigns the writings a normative role between disclosure and accountable response. **Abductive move:** I infer that the later command and recompense enact what the writings prescribe; neither later ayah explicitly says it is quoting these `كُتُب`. **Reading change:** the phrase becomes a compact statement about operative prescriptions, not merely valuable literary contents.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B007","root_001273/B008","root_001283/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d99ca3af429290fbf540","text":"2. **Plural books resisting fragmentation.** Evidence: ك ت ب B001 joining one thing to another; ق و م B007 standing in another’s place and B008 straightness; ف ر ق in 98:4 B002 scattering. Structural cue: “فيها كتب” is immediately followed by the people of the book splitting after proof. Assigned role: the books are a joined plurality held upright against later human dispersal. Abductive move: I supply the contrast that ordered plurality in revelation exposes disordered plurality among recipients. Reading change: plurality here is not confusion; it is a structured multiplicity that judges later fragmentation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B010","root_001273/B015"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d9a2e26fe33b4e0c25f9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:3:3\",\"root_001273\",[\"B010\",\"B015\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yazıların doğruluğu, sonraki en kötü ve en iyi karşıtlığında okurların kanıttan sonra yaptıklarını değerlendiren bir ölçüye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
